We are going to build a service that saves the delails of all devices as an excel sheet  per day at 11.59pm in a directory called  trips. this means that the PAI runs once a day saving the report of all devices. You will also create an API that will be consumed on the admin portal . The ApI will get the excel sheet from the trip folder , just like the saved report
